\doxysubsubsubsubsubsection{Descripción detallada}
Peripheral Control functions. 

TIM Peripheral Control functions.

\begin{DoxyVerb}  ==============================================================================
                   ##### Peripheral Control functions #####
  ==============================================================================
 [..]
   This section provides functions allowing to:
      (+) Configure The Input Output channels for OC, PWM, IC or One Pulse mode.
      (+) Configure External Clock source.
      (+) Configure Complementary channels, break features and dead time.
      (+) Configure Master and the Slave synchronization.
      (+) Configure the DMA Burst Mode.
\end{DoxyVerb}

{\em htim} & TIM handle \\
\hline
{\em Burst\+Base\+Address} & TIM Base address from where the DMA will start the Data write This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+SMCR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+DIER \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+SR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+EGR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCMR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCMR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCER \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CNT \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+PSC \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+ARR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+RCR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR3 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR4 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+BDTR \end{DoxyItemize}

Configure the DMA Burst to transfer Data from the TIM peripheral to the memory. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em htim} & TIM handle \\
\hline
{\em Burst\+Base\+Address} & TIM Base address from where the DMA will start the Data read This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+SMCR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+DIER \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+SR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+EGR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCMR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCMR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCER \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CNT \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+PSC \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+ARR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+RCR \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR1 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR2 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR3 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+CCR4 \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMABASE\+\_\+\+BDTR \end{DoxyItemize}
\\
\hline
{\em Burst\+Request\+Src} & TIM DMA Request sources This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+UPDATE: TIM update Interrupt source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+CC1: TIM Capture Compare 1 DMA source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+CC2: TIM Capture Compare 2 DMA source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+CC3: TIM Capture Compare 3 DMA source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+CC4: TIM Capture Compare 4 DMA source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+COM: TIM Commutation DMA source \item TIM\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+TRIGGER: TIM Trigger DMA source \end{DoxyItemize}
\\
\hline
{\em Burst\+Buffer} & The Buffer address. \\
\hline
{\em Burst\+Length} & DMA Burst length. This parameter can be one value between: TIM\+\_\+\+DMABURSTLENGTH\+\_\+1\+TRANSFER and TIM\+\_\+\+DMABURSTLENGTH\+\_\+18\+TRANSFERS. \\
\hline

Generate a software event. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em htim} & TIM handle \\
\hline
{\em Event\+Source} & specifies the event source. This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+UPDATE: Timer update Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+CC1: Timer Capture Compare 1 Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+CC2: Timer Capture Compare 2 Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+CC3: Timer Capture Compare 3 Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+CC4: Timer Capture Compare 4 Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+COM: Timer COM event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+TRIGGER: Timer Trigger Event source \item TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+BREAK: Timer Break event source \end{DoxyItemize}
\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
Basic timers can only generate an update event. 

TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+COM is relevant only with advanced timer instances. 

TIM\+\_\+\+EVENTSOURCE\+\_\+\+BREAK are relevant only for timer instances supporting a break input. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__def_8h_a63c0679d1cb8b8c684fbb0632743478f}{HAL\+\_\+\+Status\+Type\+Def}} HAL\+\_\+\+TIM\+\_\+\+One\+Pulse\+\_\+\+Config\+Channel (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{struct_t_i_m___handle_type_def}{TIM\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{htim}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{struct_t_i_m___one_pulse___init_type_def}{TIM\+\_\+\+One\+Pulse\+\_\+\+Init\+Type\+Def}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{s\+Config}{, }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{Output\+Channel}{, }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{Input\+Channel}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__tim_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+tim.\+h}}"{}}



Initializes the TIM One Pulse Channels according to the specified parameters in the \doxylink{struct_t_i_m___one_pulse___init_type_def}{TIM\+\_\+\+One\+Pulse\+\_\+\+Init\+Type\+Def}. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em htim} & TIM One Pulse handle \\
\hline
{\em s\+Config} & TIM One Pulse configuration structure \\
\hline
{\em Output\+Channel} & TIM output channel to configure This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+CHANNEL\+\_\+1: TIM Channel 1 selected \item TIM\+\_\+\+CHANNEL\+\_\+2: TIM Channel 2 selected \end{DoxyItemize}
\\
\hline
{\em Input\+Channel} & TIM input Channel to configure This parameter can be one of the following values: \begin{DoxyItemize}
\item TIM\+\_\+\+CHANNEL\+\_\+1: TIM Channel 1 selected \item TIM\+\_\+\+CHANNEL\+\_\+2: TIM Channel 2 selected \end{DoxyItemize}
\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
To output a waveform with a minimum delay user can enable the fast mode by calling the \doxylink{group___t_i_m___exported___macros_ga390795eb198214e5d4ed235ae3f751e4}{\+\_\+\+\_\+\+HAL\+\_\+\+TIM\+\_\+\+ENABLE\+\_\+\+OCx\+FAST} macro. Then CCx output is forced in response to the edge detection on TIx input, without taking in account the comparison. 
\end{DoxyNote}
